    var_ranges = {p0: 12, p1: 8, p2: 8}
    index0 = 64*p0 + 8*p1 + p2
    index1 = p2
    index2 = 8*p0 + p1
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf14', get_index)

        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')

    index2 = 768*p0 + p1

        load = ops.load('buf56', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')

        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')

        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index3')

        sub = ops.sub(add_1, load_3)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index3')

        constant_1 = ops.constant(1e-12, torch.float32)
        add_2 = ops.add(truediv, constant_1)
        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add_2)
        mul = ops.mul(sub, rsqrt)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_5 = ops.load('primals_34', get_index_5)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(mul, load_5)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_6 = ops.load('primals_35', get_index_6)
